# Sweep 2026-09-29: CTO / Head of Eng / VP Eng / VP AI / Founding / Principal / Staff AI / AI Architect / Forward Deployed

Bay Area, NYC and remote-US roles from the ATS boards, Wellfound, Built In and Indeed, posted in the last 30 days, five-day-onsite postings removed, already-applied/queued postings removed (69 applied, 0 queued for the local run).

**4005 new roles**: 1414 Greenhouse (auto-apply from the cloud), 1025 Ashby + 298 Lever (local run), 683 Wellfound, 1 Indeed, 584 other sites. Leadership/founding titles: 1387. AI titles: 1332. Posted pay ≥ $250K: 1837.
